#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using index_t = uint32_t;

// Constants
constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: fill
//
// Purpose:
//   Initialize array with random values
//
// Arguments:
//   A: pointer to the array to initialize
//   n: number of elements in the array
//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]
//
// ****************************************************************************
void fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));
    }
}

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: initRandomMatrix
//
// Purpose:
//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square
//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse
//   row (CSR) format.
//
// Arguments:
//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)
//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;
//                  last element is the index one past the last element
//   n:             number of nonzero elements
//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix
//
// ****************************************************************************
void initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {
    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;

    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned
    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));

    // Seed random number generator
    srand(8675309);

    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned
    bool fillRemaining = false;
    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {
        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;
        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {
            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);
            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;
            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {
                fillRemaining = true;
            }
            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;
            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {
                // Assign (i,j) a value
                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;
                nnzAssigned++;
            }
        }
    }
    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array
    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;
}

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: spmvCpu
//
// Purpose:
//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format
//
// Arguments:
//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix
//   cols: array of column indices for each element
//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;
//                  last element is the index one past the last element
//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication
//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix
//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation
//
// ****************************************************************************
void spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,
             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {
        double t = 0.0;
        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {
            const auto col = cols[j];
            t += val[j] * vec[col];
        }
        out[i] = t;
    }
}

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: verifyResults
//
// Purpose:
//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution
//
// Arguments:
//   reference: array holding the reference result vector
//   result: array holding the result vector to verify
//   size: number of elements per vector
//
// ****************************************************************************
bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
        const double ref = reference[i];
        const double res = result[i];
        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
            // For very small values, check absolute error
            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\n", i, ref, res);
                return false;
            }
        } else {
            // Check relative error
            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);
            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, ref, res, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    index_t numRows = 1024;
    index_t sparsity = 10;
    index_t iterations = 10;
    double maxVal = 1.0;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;

    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-m") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }

    // Calculate number of non-zero elements
    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;

    printf("Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %u x %u\n", numRows, numRows);
    printf("Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\n", sparsity);
    printf("Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\n", 
           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));
    printf("Iterations: %u\n", iterations);
    printf("Max value: %.2f\n", maxVal);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");

    // Allocate and initialize data structures
    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems);                  // Non-zero values
    std::vector<index_t> h_cols(nItems);                // Column indices
    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters(numRows + 1);  // Row delimiters
    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);                 // Dense vector
    std::vector<double> h_out(numRows);                 // Output vector

    printf("Initializing data structures...\n");
    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);
    fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);
    initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);

    // For validation, compute reference solution
    std::vector<double> h_reference;
    if (validate) {
        printf("Computing reference solution...\n");
        h_reference.resize(numRows);
        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), 
                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());
    }

    // Perform SpMV computation
    printf("Computing SpMV...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),
                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_out.data());
    }

    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);

    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    const double avgTime = duration.count() / static_cast<double>(iterations);
    
    printf("Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\n", avgTime);
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(h_out, "OutputVector");
    }

    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);

        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }

    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

+// One warp computes one CSR row.  Consecutive lanes consume consecutive CSR
+// entries, which coalesces the matrix reads; the reduction stays entirely in
+// registers and requires no block-wide synchronization or shared memory.
+__global__ void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                              const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                              const index_t numRows,
+                              double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * WARPS_PER_BLOCK +
+                        static_cast<index_t>(threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE);
+
+    if (row >= numRows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const index_t rowBegin = rowDelimiters[row];
+    const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+    for (index_t entry = rowBegin + static_cast<index_t>(lane); entry < rowEnd;
+         entry += WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum = fma(val[entry], __ldg(&vec[cols[entry]]), sum);
+    }
+
+    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+

+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const char* file, const int line) {

+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);

     }

+// One warp computes one CSR row.  Consecutive lanes consume consecutive CSR
+// entries, which coalesces the matrix reads; the reduction stays entirely in
+// registers and requires no block-wide synchronization or shared memory.
+__global__ void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                              const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                              const index_t numRows,
+                              double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * WARPS_PER_BLOCK +
+                        static_cast<index_t>(threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE);
+
+    if (row >= numRows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const index_t rowBegin = rowDelimiters[row];
+    const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+    for (index_t entry = rowBegin + static_cast<index_t>(lane); entry < rowEnd;
+         entry += WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum = fma(val[entry], __ldg(&vec[cols[entry]]), sum);
+    }
+
+    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+

 // Function: verifyResults
 //

+set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD 20)
+set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
+
+# Compile for the GPU present on the build machine unless the caller selects a
+# different architecture explicitly.  This avoids a runtime JIT and permits
+# the compiler to use the native GPU instruction set.
+if (NOT DEFINED CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES)
+    set(CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native)
+endif()
 
 add_executable(spmv spmv.cpp)
+set_source_files_properties(spmv.cpp PROPERTIES LANGUAGE CUDA)

+    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CXX>:-O3;-march=native;-Wall;-Wextra>
+    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-O3>
+)
 
-# Link math library
-target_link_libraries(spmv m)
+set_target_properties(spmv PROPERTIES

 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
 using index_t = uint32_t;
 
 // Constants
 constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
+constexpr int WARP_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr int WARPS_PER_BLOCK = 8;
+constexpr int THREADS_PER_BLOCK = WARP_SIZE * WARPS_PER_BLOCK;
+
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
+                     file, line, operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) checkCuda((operation), #operation, __FILE__, __LINE__)
 
 // ****************************************************************************
 // Function: fill
@@ -105,6 +120,41 @@
     }
 }
 
+// One warp computes one CSR row.  Consecutive lanes consume consecutive CSR
+// entries, which coalesces the matrix reads; the reduction stays entirely in
+// registers and requires no block-wide synchronization or shared memory.
+__global__ void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                              const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                              const index_t numRows,
+                              double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * WARPS_PER_BLOCK +
+                        static_cast<index_t>(threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE);
+
+    if (row >= numRows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const index_t rowBegin = rowDelimiters[row];
+    const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+    for (index_t entry = rowBegin + static_cast<index_t>(lane); entry < rowEnd;
+         entry += WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum = fma(val[entry], __ldg(&vec[cols[entry]]), sum);
+    }
+
+    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
 // ****************************************************************************
 // Function: verifyResults
 //
@@ -217,23 +267,77 @@
                 h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());
     }
 
-    // Perform SpMV computation
+    // Copy the CSR matrix and dense vector to the GPU once.  All timed
+    // iterations then run entirely on the device.
+    double* d_val = nullptr;
+    index_t* d_cols = nullptr;
+    index_t* d_rowDelimiters = nullptr;
+    double* d_vec = nullptr;
+    double* d_out = nullptr;
+
+    const size_t valBytes = static_cast<size_t>(nItems) * sizeof(*d_val);
+    const size_t colBytes = static_cast<size_t>(nItems) * sizeof(*d_cols);
+    const size_t rowDelimiterBytes = static_cast<size_t>(numRows + 1) * sizeof(*d_rowDelimiters);
+    const size_t vectorBytes = static_cast<size_t>(numRows) * sizeof(*d_vec);
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_val), valBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_cols), colBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_rowDelimiters), rowDelimiterBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_vec), vectorBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_out), vectorBytes));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_val, h_val.data(), valBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_cols, h_cols.data(), colBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_rowDelimiters, h_rowDelimiters.data(), rowDelimiterBytes,
+                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_vec, h_vec.data(), vectorBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+    // Perform SpMV computation. CUDA events measure only GPU execution time,
+    // matching the original benchmark's intent to exclude setup work.
     printf("Computing SpMV...\n");
-    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
+    cudaEvent_t startEvent;
+    cudaEvent_t stopEvent;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&startEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stopEvent));
+
+    const dim3 block(THREADS_PER_BLOCK);
+    const dim3 grid((numRows + WARPS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / WARPS_PER_BLOCK);
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(startEvent));
 
-    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
-        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(),
-                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_out.data());
+    if (numRows != 0) {
+        for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            spmvCsrKernel<<<grid, block>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, numRows, d_out);
+        }
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
     }
 
-    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
-    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stopEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stopEvent));
+
+    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0f;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, startEvent, stopEvent));
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_out.data(), d_out, vectorBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
 
-    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_out));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_vec));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_rowDelimiters));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cols));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_val));
+
+    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds));
     
     // Calculate performance metrics
-    const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
-    const double avgTime = duration.count() / static_cast<double>(iterations);
+    const double gflops = elapsedMilliseconds > 0.0f
+                               ? (2.0 * nItems * iterations) /
+                                     (static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds) / 1000.0) / 1e9
+                               : 0.0;
+    const double avgTime = iterations != 0
+                               ? static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds) / static_cast<double>(iterations)
+                               : 0.0;
     
     printf("Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\n", avgTime);
     printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);

+constexpr int WARPS_PER_BLOCK = 8;
+constexpr int THREADS_PER_BLOCK = WARP_SIZE * WARPS_PER_BLOCK;
+
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
+                     file, line, operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) checkCuda((operation), #operation, __FILE__, __LINE__)
 
 // ****************************************************************************
 // Function: fill
@@ -105,6 +120,41 @@
     }
 }
 
+// One warp computes one CSR row.  Consecutive lanes consume consecutive CSR
+// entries, which coalesces the matrix reads; the reduction stays entirely in
+// registers and requires no block-wide synchronization or shared memory.
+__global__ void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                              const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                              const index_t numRows,
+                              double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * WARPS_PER_BLOCK +
+                        static_cast<index_t>(threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE);
+
+    if (row >= numRows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const index_t rowBegin = rowDelimiters[row];
+    const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+    for (index_t entry = rowBegin + static_cast<index_t>(lane); entry < rowEnd;
+         entry += WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum = fma(val[entry], __ldg(&vec[cols[entry]]), sum);
+    }
+
+    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+

 //

+            spmvCsrKernel<<<grid, block>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, numRows, d_out);

+constexpr int WARP_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr int WARPS_PER_BLOCK = 8;
+constexpr int THREADS_PER_BLOCK = WARP_SIZE * WARPS_PER_BLOCK;
+
+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
+                     file, line, operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}

 // ****************************************************************************
 // Function: fill
@@ -105,6 +120,41 @@
     }
 }
 
+// One warp computes one CSR row.  Consecutive lanes consume consecutive CSR
+// entries, which coalesces the matrix reads; the reduction stays entirely in
+// registers and requires no block-wide synchronization or shared memory.
+__global__ void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                              const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                              const index_t numRows,
+                              double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * WARPS_PER_BLOCK +
+                        static_cast<index_t>(threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE);
+
+    if (row >= numRows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const index_t rowBegin = rowDelimiters[row];
+    const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+    for (index_t entry = rowBegin + static_cast<index_t>(lane); entry < rowEnd;
+         entry += WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum = fma(val[entry], __ldg(&vec[cols[entry]]), sum);
+    }
+
+    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
 // ****************************************************************************
 // Function: verifyResults
 //

+    const size_t vectorBytes = static_cast<size_t>(numRows) * sizeof(*d_vec);

+// One warp computes one CSR row.  Consecutive lanes consume consecutive CSR
+// entries, which coalesces the matrix reads; the reduction stays entirely in
+// registers and requires no block-wide synchronization or shared memory.
+__global__ void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                              const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                              const index_t numRows,
+                              double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * WARPS_PER_BLOCK +
+                        static_cast<index_t>(threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE);
+
+    if (row >= numRows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const index_t rowBegin = rowDelimiters[row];
+    const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+    for (index_t entry = rowBegin + static_cast<index_t>(lane); entry < rowEnd;
+         entry += WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum = fma(val[entry], __ldg(&vec[cols[entry]]), sum);
+    }
+
+    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}

#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include <cuda_runtime.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using index_t = uint32_t;

// Constants
constexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;
constexpr int WARP_SIZE = 32;
constexpr int WARPS_PER_BLOCK = 8;
constexpr int THREADS_PER_BLOCK = WARP_SIZE * WARPS_PER_BLOCK;

void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const char* file, const int line) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
                     file, line, operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
    }
}

#define CUDA_CHECK(operation) checkCuda((operation), #operation, __FILE__, __LINE__)

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: fill
//
// Purpose:
//   Initialize array with random values
//
// Arguments:
//   A: pointer to the array to initialize
//   n: number of elements in the array
//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]
//
// ****************************************************************************
void fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));
    }
}

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: initRandomMatrix
//
// Purpose:
//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square
//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse
//   row (CSR) format.
//
// Arguments:
//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)
//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;
//                  last element is the index one past the last element
//   n:             number of nonzero elements
//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix
//
// ****************************************************************************
void initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {
    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;

    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned
    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));

    // Seed random number generator
    srand(8675309);

    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned
    bool fillRemaining = false;
    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {
        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;
        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {
            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);
            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;
            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {
                fillRemaining = true;
            }
            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;
            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {
                // Assign (i,j) a value
                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;
                nnzAssigned++;
            }
        }
    }
    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array
    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;
}

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: spmvCpu
//
// Purpose:
//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format
//
// Arguments:
//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix
//   cols: array of column indices for each element
//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;
//                  last element is the index one past the last element
//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication
//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix
//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation
//
// ****************************************************************************
void spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,
             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {
        double t = 0.0;
        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {
            const auto col = cols[j];
            t += val[j] * vec[col];
        }
        out[i] = t;
    }
}

// One warp computes one CSR row.  Consecutive lanes consume consecutive CSR
// entries, which coalesces the matrix reads; the reduction stays entirely in
// registers and requires no block-wide synchronization or shared memory.
__global__ void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
                              const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
                              const double* __restrict__ vec,
                              const index_t numRows,
                              double* __restrict__ out) {
    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * WARPS_PER_BLOCK +
                        static_cast<index_t>(threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE);

    if (row >= numRows) {
        return;
    }

    const index_t rowBegin = rowDelimiters[row];
    const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
    double sum = 0.0;

    for (index_t entry = rowBegin + static_cast<index_t>(lane); entry < rowEnd;
         entry += WARP_SIZE) {
        sum = fma(val[entry], __ldg(&vec[cols[entry]]), sum);
    }

    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
    }

    if (lane == 0) {
        out[row] = sum;
    }
}

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: verifyResults
//
// Purpose:
//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution
//
// Arguments:
//   reference: array holding the reference result vector
//   result: array holding the result vector to verify
//   size: number of elements per vector
//
// ****************************************************************************
bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
        const double ref = reference[i];
        const double res = result[i];
        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
            // For very small values, check absolute error
            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\n", i, ref, res);
                return false;
            }
        } else {
            // Check relative error
            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);
            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, ref, res, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    index_t numRows = 1024;
    index_t sparsity = 10;
    index_t iterations = 10;
    double maxVal = 1.0;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;

    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-m") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }

    // Calculate number of non-zero elements
    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;

    printf("Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %u x %u\n", numRows, numRows);
    printf("Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\n", sparsity);
    printf("Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\n", 
           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));
    printf("Iterations: %u\n", iterations);
    printf("Max value: %.2f\n", maxVal);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");

    // Allocate and initialize data structures
    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems);                  // Non-zero values
    std::vector<index_t> h_cols(nItems);                // Column indices
    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters(numRows + 1);  // Row delimiters
    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);                 // Dense vector
    std::vector<double> h_out(numRows);                 // Output vector

    printf("Initializing data structures...\n");
    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);
    fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);
    initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);

    // For validation, compute reference solution
    std::vector<double> h_reference;
    if (validate) {
        printf("Computing reference solution...\n");
        h_reference.resize(numRows);
        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), 
                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());
    }

    // Copy the CSR matrix and dense vector to the GPU once.  All timed
    // iterations then run entirely on the device.
    double* d_val = nullptr;
    index_t* d_cols = nullptr;
    index_t* d_rowDelimiters = nullptr;
    double* d_vec = nullptr;
    double* d_out = nullptr;

    const size_t valBytes = static_cast<size_t>(nItems) * sizeof(*d_val);
    const size_t colBytes = static_cast<size_t>(nItems) * sizeof(*d_cols);
    const size_t rowDelimiterBytes = static_cast<size_t>(numRows + 1) * sizeof(*d_rowDelimiters);
    const size_t vectorBytes = static_cast<size_t>(numRows) * sizeof(*d_vec);

    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_val), valBytes));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_cols), colBytes));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_rowDelimiters), rowDelimiterBytes));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_vec), vectorBytes));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_out), vectorBytes));

    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_val, h_val.data(), valBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_cols, h_cols.data(), colBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_rowDelimiters, h_rowDelimiters.data(), rowDelimiterBytes,
                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_vec, h_vec.data(), vectorBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
    // Preserve the host vector's value-initialized behavior when zero
    // benchmark iterations are requested.
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_out, 0, vectorBytes));

    // Perform SpMV computation. CUDA events measure only GPU execution time,
    // matching the original benchmark's intent to exclude setup work.
    printf("Computing SpMV...\n");
    cudaEvent_t startEvent;
    cudaEvent_t stopEvent;
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&startEvent));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stopEvent));

    const dim3 block(THREADS_PER_BLOCK);
    const dim3 grid((numRows + WARPS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / WARPS_PER_BLOCK);

    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(startEvent));

    if (numRows != 0) {
        for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
            spmvCsrKernel<<<grid, block>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, numRows, d_out);
        }
        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
    }

    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stopEvent));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stopEvent));

    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0f;
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, startEvent, stopEvent));

    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_out.data(), d_out, vectorBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));

    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_out));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_vec));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_rowDelimiters));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cols));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_val));

    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds));
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    const double gflops = elapsedMilliseconds > 0.0f
                               ? (2.0 * nItems * iterations) /
                                     (static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds) / 1000.0) / 1e9
                               : 0.0;
    const double avgTime = iterations != 0
                               ? static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds) / static_cast<double>(iterations)
                               : 0.0;
    
    printf("Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\n", avgTime);
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(h_out, "OutputVector");
    }

    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        const bool valid = verifyResults(h_reference.data(), h_out.data(), numRows);

        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;

# different architecture explicitly.  This avoids a runtime JIT and permits
# the compiler to use the native GPU instruction set.

    set(CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native)
endif()

    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CXX>:-O3;-march=native;-Wall;-Wextra>
    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-O3>
)

    CUDA_SEPARABLE_COMPILATION OFF

+constexpr int WARP_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr int WARPS_PER_BLOCK = 8;
+constexpr int THREADS_PER_BLOCK = WARP_SIZE * WARPS_PER_BLOCK;

+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
+                     file, line, operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);

+// One warp computes one CSR row.  Consecutive lanes consume consecutive CSR
+// entries, which coalesces the matrix reads; the reduction stays entirely in
+// registers and requires no block-wide synchronization or shared memory.
+__global__ void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                              const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                              const index_t numRows,
+                              double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * WARPS_PER_BLOCK +
+                        static_cast<index_t>(threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE);
+
+    if (row >= numRows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const index_t rowBegin = rowDelimiters[row];
+    const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+    for (index_t entry = rowBegin + static_cast<index_t>(lane); entry < rowEnd;
+         entry += WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum = fma(val[entry], __ldg(&vec[cols[entry]]), sum);
+    }
+
+    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
 // ****************************************************************************
 // Function: verifyResults
 //

codex
Validation passes for dense, sparse, tiny, zero-nonzero, and zero-iteration cases. The CUDA kernel uses warp-level reductions with coalesced CSR reads, and its timing covers only the repeated device computation.

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const char* file, const int line) {

+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);

+// One warp computes one CSR row.  Consecutive lanes consume consecutive CSR
+// entries, which coalesces the matrix reads; the reduction stays entirely in
+// registers and requires no block-wide synchronization or shared memory.
+__global__ void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                              const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                              const index_t numRows,
+                              double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * WARPS_PER_BLOCK +
+                        static_cast<index_t>(threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE);
+
+    if (row >= numRows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const index_t rowBegin = rowDelimiters[row];
+    const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+    for (index_t entry = rowBegin + static_cast<index_t>(lane); entry < rowEnd;
+         entry += WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum = fma(val[entry], __ldg(&vec[cols[entry]]), sum);
+    }
+
+    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
 // ****************************************************************************
 // Function: verifyResults
 //

codex
I found CMake’s `native` auto-detection targeting an old virtual architecture (`sm_52`) despite the available RTX 3090s. I’m pinning the default build to Ampere `sm_86` (while retaining a CMake override) so the generated kernel is actually optimized for the target GPU.
apply patch

+// One warp computes one CSR row.  Consecutive lanes consume consecutive CSR
+// entries, which coalesces the matrix reads; the reduction stays entirely in
+// registers and requires no block-wide synchronization or shared memory.
+__global__ void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                              const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                              const index_t numRows,
+                              double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * WARPS_PER_BLOCK +
+                        static_cast<index_t>(threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE);
+
+    if (row >= numRows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const index_t rowBegin = rowDelimiters[row];
+    const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+    for (index_t entry = rowBegin + static_cast<index_t>(lane); entry < rowEnd;
+         entry += WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum = fma(val[entry], __ldg(&vec[cols[entry]]), sum);
+    }
+
+    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
 // ****************************************************************************
 // Function: verifyResults
 //

+// registers and requires no block-wide synchronization or shared memory.
+__global__ void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                              const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                              const index_t numRows,
+                              double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * WARPS_PER_BLOCK +
+                        static_cast<index_t>(threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE);
+
+    if (row >= numRows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const index_t rowBegin = rowDelimiters[row];
+    const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+    for (index_t entry = rowBegin + static_cast<index_t>(lane); entry < rowEnd;
+         entry += WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum = fma(val[entry], __ldg(&vec[cols[entry]]), sum);
+    }
+
+    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
 // ****************************************************************************
 // Function: verifyResults
 //

+    index_t* d_rowDelimiters = nullptr;
+    double* d_vec = nullptr;
+    double* d_out = nullptr;
+

+    const size_t colBytes = static_cast<size_t>(nItems) * sizeof(*d_cols);
+    const size_t rowDelimiterBytes = static_cast<size_t>(numRows + 1) * sizeof(*d_rowDelimiters);
+    const size_t vectorBytes = static_cast<size_t>(numRows) * sizeof(*d_vec);
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_val), valBytes));

+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_rowDelimiters), rowDelimiterBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_vec), vectorBytes));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_out), vectorBytes));
+

+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_cols, h_cols.data(), colBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_rowDelimiters, h_rowDelimiters.data(), rowDelimiterBytes,

cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.24)

set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

# RTX 3090-class Ampere GPUs use compute capability 8.6.  Callers targeting a
# different GPU can still override CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES at configure time.
# Supplying a real architecture avoids runtime JIT and enables Ampere codegen.
if (NOT DEFINED CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES)
    set(CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES 86)
endif()

add_executable(spmv spmv.cpp)
set_source_files_properties(spmv.cpp PROPERTIES LANGUAGE CUDA)

# Enable aggressive optimization for both host setup code and the CUDA kernel.
target_compile_options(spmv PRIVATE
    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CXX>:-O3;-march=native;-Wall;-Wextra>
    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-O3>
)

set_target_properties(spmv PROPERTIES
    CUDA_SEPARABLE_COMPILATION OFF
)

+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
+                     file, line, operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);

+// One warp computes one CSR row.  Consecutive lanes consume consecutive CSR
+// entries, which coalesces the matrix reads; the reduction stays entirely in
+// registers and requires no block-wide synchronization or shared memory.
+__global__ void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                              const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                              const index_t numRows,
+                              double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * WARPS_PER_BLOCK +
+                        static_cast<index_t>(threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE);
+
+    if (row >= numRows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const index_t rowBegin = rowDelimiters[row];
+    const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+    for (index_t entry = rowBegin + static_cast<index_t>(lane); entry < rowEnd;
+         entry += WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum = fma(val[entry], __ldg(&vec[cols[entry]]), sum);
+    }
+
+    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
 // ****************************************************************************
 // Function: verifyResults
 //

+void checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation, const char* file, const int line) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
+                     file, line, operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }

     }

+// One warp computes one CSR row.  Consecutive lanes consume consecutive CSR
+// entries, which coalesces the matrix reads; the reduction stays entirely in
+// registers and requires no block-wide synchronization or shared memory.
+__global__ void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                              const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                              const index_t numRows,
+                              double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * WARPS_PER_BLOCK +
+                        static_cast<index_t>(threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE);
+
+    if (row >= numRows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const index_t rowBegin = rowDelimiters[row];
+    const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+    for (index_t entry = rowBegin + static_cast<index_t>(lane); entry < rowEnd;
+         entry += WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum = fma(val[entry], __ldg(&vec[cols[entry]]), sum);
+    }
+
+    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+
 // ****************************************************************************
 // Function: verifyResults
 //

    55	//
    56	// Arguments:
    57	//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)
    58	//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;
    59	//                  last element is the index one past the last element
    60	//   n:             number of nonzero elements
    61	//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix
    62	//
    63	// ****************************************************************************
    64	void initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {
    65	    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;
    66	
    67	    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned
    68	    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));
    69	
    70	    // Seed random number generator
    71	    srand(8675309);
    72	
    73	    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned
    74	    bool fillRemaining = false;
    75	    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {
    76	        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;
    77	        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {
    78	            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);
    79	            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;
    80	            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {
    81	                fillRemaining = true;
    82	            }
    83	            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;
    84	            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {
    85	                // Assign (i,j) a value
    86	                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;
    87	                nnzAssigned++;
    88	            }
    89	        }
    90	    }
    91	    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array
    92	    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;
    93	}
    94	
    95	// ****************************************************************************
    96	// Function: spmvCpu
    97	//
    98	// Purpose:
    99	//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format
   100	//
   101	// Arguments:
   102	//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix
   103	//   cols: array of column indices for each element
   104	//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;
   105	//                  last element is the index one past the last element
   106	//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication
   107	//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix
   108	//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation
   109	//
   110	// ****************************************************************************
   111	void spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,
   112	             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {
   113	    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {
   114	        double t = 0.0;
   115	        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {
   116	            const auto col = cols[j];
   117	            t += val[j] * vec[col];
   118	        }
   119	        out[i] = t;
   120	    }
   121	}
   122	
   123	// One warp computes one CSR row.  Consecutive lanes consume consecutive CSR
   124	// entries, which coalesces the matrix reads; the reduction stays entirely in
   125	// registers and requires no block-wide synchronization or shared memory.
   126	__global__ void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
   127	                              const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
   128	                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
   129	                              const double* __restrict__ vec,
   130	                              const index_t numRows,
   131	                              double* __restrict__ out) {
   132	    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
   133	    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * WARPS_PER_BLOCK +
   134	                        static_cast<index_t>(threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE);
   135	
   136	    if (row >= numRows) {
   137	        return;
   138	    }
   139	
   140	    const index_t rowBegin = rowDelimiters[row];
   141	    const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
   142	    double sum = 0.0;
   143	
   144	    for (index_t entry = rowBegin + static_cast<index_t>(lane); entry < rowEnd;
   145	         entry += WARP_SIZE) {
   146	        sum = fma(val[entry], __ldg(&vec[cols[entry]]), sum);
   147	    }
   148	
   149	    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
   150	        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
   151	    }
   152	
   153	    if (lane == 0) {
   154	        out[row] = sum;
   155	    }
   156	}
   157	
   158	// ****************************************************************************
   159	// Function: verifyResults
   160	//
   161	// Purpose:
   162	//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution
   163	//
   164	// Arguments:
   165	//   reference: array holding the reference result vector
   166	//   result: array holding the result vector to verify
   167	//   size: number of elements per vector
   168	//
   169	// ****************************************************************************
   170	bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {
   171	    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
   172	        const double ref = reference[i];
   173	        const double res = result[i];
   174	        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
   175	            // For very small values, check absolute error
   176	            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
   177	                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\n", i, ref, res);
   178	                return false;
   179	            }
   180	        } else {
   181	            // Check relative error
   182	            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);
   183	            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
   184	                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\n",
   185	                       i, ref, res, relError);
   186	                return false;
   187	            }
   188	        }
   189	    }
   190	    return true;
   191	}
   192	
   193	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   194	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   195	    printf("Options:\n");
   196	    printf("  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\n");
   197	    printf("  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\n");
   198	    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
   199	    printf("  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\n");
   200	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   201	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   202	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   203	}
   204	
   205	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   206	    index_t numRows = 1024;
   207	    index_t sparsity = 10;
   208	    index_t iterations = 10;
   209	    double maxVal = 1.0;
   210	    bool validate = false;
   211	    bool printResults = false;
   212	
   213	    // Parse command line arguments
   214	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   215	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   216	            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
   217	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   218	            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
   219	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   220	            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));
   221	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-m") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   222	            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);
   223	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   224	            validate = true;
   225	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   226	            printResults = true;
   227	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   228	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   229	            return 0;
   230	        } else {
   231	            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   232	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   233	            return 1;
   234	        }
   235	    }
   236	
   237	    // Calculate number of non-zero elements
   238	    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;
   239	
   240	    printf("Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\n");
   241	    printf("Matrix size: %u x %u\n", numRows, numRows);
   242	    printf("Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\n", sparsity);
   243	    printf("Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\n", 
   244	           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));
   245	    printf("Iterations: %u\n", iterations);
   246	    printf("Max value: %.2f\n", maxVal);
   247	    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   248	
   249	    // Allocate and initialize data structures
   250	    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems);                  // Non-zero values
   251	    std::vector<index_t> h_cols(nItems);                // Column indices
   252	    std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters(numRows + 1);  // Row delimiters
   253	    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);                 // Dense vector
   254	    std::vector<double> h_out(numRows);                 // Output vector
   255	
   256	    printf("Initializing data structures...\n");
   257	    fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);
   258	    fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);
   259	    initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);
   260	
   261	    // For validation, compute reference solution
   262	    std::vector<double> h_reference;
   263	    if (validate) {
   264	        printf("Computing reference solution...\n");
   265	        h_reference.resize(numRows);
   266	        spmvCpu(h_val.data(), h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), 
   267	                h_vec.data(), numRows, h_reference.data());
   268	    }
   269	
   270	    // Copy the CSR matrix and dense vector to the GPU once.  All timed
   271	    // iterations then run entirely on the device.
   272	    double* d_val = nullptr;
   273	    index_t* d_cols = nullptr;
   274	    index_t* d_rowDelimiters = nullptr;
   275	    double* d_vec = nullptr;
   276	    double* d_out = nullptr;
   277	
   278	    const size_t valBytes = static_cast<size_t>(nItems) * sizeof(*d_val);
   279	    const size_t colBytes = static_cast<size_t>(nItems) * sizeof(*d_cols);
   280	    const size_t rowDelimiterBytes = static_cast<size_t>(numRows + 1) * sizeof(*d_rowDelimiters);
   281	    const size_t vectorBytes = static_cast<size_t>(numRows) * sizeof(*d_vec);
   282	
   283	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_val), valBytes));
   284	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_cols), colBytes));
   285	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_rowDelimiters), rowDelimiterBytes));
   286	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_vec), vectorBytes));
   287	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&d_out), vectorBytes));
   288	
   289	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_val, h_val.data(), valBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   290	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_cols, h_cols.data(), colBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   291	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_rowDelimiters, h_rowDelimiters.data(), rowDelimiterBytes,
   292	                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   293	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_vec, h_vec.data(), vectorBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   294	    // Preserve the host vector's value-initialized behavior when zero
   295	    // benchmark iterations are requested.
   296	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_out, 0, vectorBytes));
   297	
   298	    // Perform SpMV computation. CUDA events measure only GPU execution time,
   299	    // matching the original benchmark's intent to exclude setup work.
   300	    printf("Computing SpMV...\n");
   301	    cudaEvent_t startEvent;
   302	    cudaEvent_t stopEvent;
   303	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&startEvent));
   304	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stopEvent));
   305	
   306	    const dim3 block(THREADS_PER_BLOCK);
   307	    const dim3 grid((numRows + WARPS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / WARPS_PER_BLOCK);
   308	
   309	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(startEvent));
   310	
   311	    if (numRows != 0) {
   312	        for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
   313	            spmvCsrKernel<<<grid, block>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, numRows, d_out);
   314	        }
   315	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   316	    }
   317	
   318	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stopEvent));
   319	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stopEvent));
   320	
   321	    float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0f;
   322	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, startEvent, stopEvent));
   323	
   324	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_out.data(), d_out, vectorBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   325	
   326	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(startEvent));
   327	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent));
   328	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_out));
   329	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_vec));
   330	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_rowDelimiters));
   331	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cols));
   332	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_val));
   333	
   334	    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds));
   335	    
   336	    // Calculate performance metrics
   337	    const double gflops = elapsedMilliseconds > 0.0f
   338	                               ? (2.0 * nItems * iterations) /
   339	                                     (static_cast<double>(elapsedMilliseconds) / 1000.0) / 1e9
   340	                               : 0.0;
     1	cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.24)
     2	
     3	# RTX 3090-class Ampere GPUs use compute capability 8.6.  Callers targeting a
     4	# different GPU can still override CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES at configure time.
     5	# Supplying a real architecture avoids runtime JIT and enables Ampere codegen.
     6	if (NOT DEFINED CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES)
     7	    set(CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES 86)
     8	endif()
     9

    23	    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-O3>
    24	)

    27	    CUDA_SEPARABLE_COMPILATION OFF
    28	)

+// One warp computes one CSR row.  Consecutive lanes consume consecutive CSR
+// entries, which coalesces the matrix reads; the reduction stays entirely in
+// registers and requires no block-wide synchronization or shared memory.
+__global__ void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                              const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                              const index_t numRows,
+                              double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * WARPS_PER_BLOCK +
+                        static_cast<index_t>(threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE);
+
+    if (row >= numRows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const index_t rowBegin = rowDelimiters[row];
+    const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+    double sum = 0.0;
+

+         entry += WARP_SIZE) {

+    }
+

+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+

+        out[row] = sum;
+    }

     }

+// One warp computes one CSR row.  Consecutive lanes consume consecutive CSR
+// entries, which coalesces the matrix reads; the reduction stays entirely in
+// registers and requires no block-wide synchronization or shared memory.
+__global__ void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                              const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                              const index_t numRows,
+                              double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (WARP_SIZE - 1);
+    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * WARPS_PER_BLOCK +
+                        static_cast<index_t>(threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE);
+
+    if (row >= numRows) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const index_t rowBegin = rowDelimiters[row];
+    const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+    double sum = 0.0;
+
+    for (index_t entry = rowBegin + static_cast<index_t>(lane); entry < rowEnd;
+         entry += WARP_SIZE) {
+        sum = fma(val[entry], __ldg(&vec[cols[entry]]), sum);
+    }
+
+    for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+        sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+    }
+
+    if (lane == 0) {
+        out[row] = sum;
+    }
+}
+

 //
